ATO ORDINATÓRIO

Providencie o autor, no prazo de 15 dias úteis, o recolhimento das custas
iniciais, sob pena de cancelamento da distribuição, nos termos do Artigo
290 do CPC. Fica a parte autora intimada a proceder também ao recolhimento
da taxa de postagem ou diligência ao Sr. Oficial de Justiça, para posterior
expedição de carta/mandado para citação/intimação.

Anoto que no EPROC cabe ao advogado cadastrar a procuração diretamente no
sistema, não bastando a sua mera juntada aos autos.

A eventual correção de tais dados deve ser realizada diretamente pelo
procurador mediante acesso ao sistema, lançando-se o evento "PROCURAÇÃO".

A procuração não será cadastrada, caso o evento lançado seja diferente de
"PROCURAÇÃO", ou seja, caso lançado o evento como PETIÇÃO ou DOCUMENTO, não
será gerada a correta movimentação processual.

Com a regularização do cadastro do advogado será gerado no processo um
evento correspondente, permitindo que o processo receba tramitação.

Para mais informações de como providenciar o cadastro, a patrono poderá
acessar o INFOEPROC nº 55,
https://www.tjsp.jus.br/Download/EPROC/InfoEproc/Infoeproc55.pdf?d=638948241174021983

No caso de substabelecimento, o procurador deverá o procurador realizar a
sua regularização diretamente no sistema E-PROC, conforme o seguinte
tutorial INFOEPROC nº 20, que pode ser consultado diretamente no site do
Tribunal:
https://www.tjsp.jus.br/Download/EPROC/InfoEproc/Infoeproc20.pdf?d=1761678562036

Local: Indaiatuba

Judge’s Amendment Request – Including Movida & Additional Requirements

New Court Order and Current Case Status

Yes – this 15-day amendment request is a new development in your case
(Jesse Daniel Brown vs. Seguros Sura S.A.). The judge identified issues
with the initial filing and, on 04/11/2025, ordered you (the plaintiff) to
fix them within 15 days under penalty of case dismissal

Gmail

. This order was published on 05/11/2025, so the clock started once you
were officially intimated (the deadline runs from 07/11/2025 to 01/12/2025
as per the docket). We have not seen this exact request before in this case
– it’s essentially the first judicial review of your complaint. In summary,
the court’s dispatch (despacho) says:

Provide a proper power of attorney (procuração) for your lawyer in this
case (the previous one from 2024 was tied to another case)

Gmail

. This must be uploaded in the e-proc system using the specific
“PROCURAÇÃO” event, not just attached as a random document.

Attach copies of your personal identification documents (ID/passport/RNE
and CPF)

Gmail

.

Attach a proof of address (utility bill or similar, from last 90 days)

Gmail

.

**Include “Movida Locação de Veículos S.A.” as a defendant in the lawsuit
(add them to the polo passivo)

Gmail

.

Submit any available evidence supporting your claims (at least the
essential proof you already have).

Failing to meet these requirements in time can lead to the dismissal
(extinção) of your case without judgment on the merits, per Article 321 of
the Brazilian CPC which gives 15 days for the plaintiff to amend the
initial petition as directed

jusbrasil.com.br

. So it’s crucial to comply with all items by the deadline.

Why the Judge Requires Including Movida S.A. as Defendant

The judge specifically noted the absence of Movida Locação de Veículos S.A.
as a defendant and is requiring its inclusion. This is because Movida is
directly involved in the underlying dispute: the insurance claim arises
from an accident involving a Movida rental car. In fact, the insurance
policy (Seguros Sura) was issued to cover Movida’s vehicle – making Movida
the insured party in this claim

Google Drive

. You have been dealing with Sura as a third-party claimant (a victim of an
accident involving Movida’s car), but under Brazilian law it’s often
necessary to have the insured party in the lawsuit when suing the insurer.
This ensures all interested parties to the contract are before the court.
In practice, some jurisdictions allow direct action against the insurer
alone, but many judges (like yours) prefer or require a “litisconsórcio
passivo necessário” – i.e. joining the insured (Movida) as a co-defendant –
to avoid future procedural issues. In short, Movida’s presence is needed to
validate any judgment: for example, Sura could later argue they can’t be
held liable because the policyholder wasn’t part of the suit. Including
Movida now cures that potential defect

Gmail

.

How to Add Movida to the Lawsuit (Amending the Complaint)

To include Movida as a defendant, your attorney must file an emenda à
petição inicial (amendment to the initial complaint) through the
e-proc/TJSP system. This is a straightforward petition referencing the case
number and explaining the amendment. Key steps and content will include:

Caption and Parties: Update the case caption to add Movida Locação de
Veículos S.A. alongside Seguros Sura S.A. as defendants. Provide Movida’s
identifying details: its full legal name, CNPJ 07.976.147/0001-60, and a
service address. Typically the headquarters address is used – for Movida,
one known address is Rua Dr. Renato Paes de Barros, 1017, Conj. 92, Itaim
Bibi, São Paulo – SP, CEP 04530-001 (their corporate office)

econodata.com.br

cnpj.info

. Including the CNPJ and address is important so the court can issue a
summons to Movida at the correct location

Gmail

. (Your lawyer will know to do this, but it’s good to have the info ready.)

Body of the Petition: Briefly state that you are amending the initial
complaint per the court’s order to join Movida as a defendant. You can
explain why Movida is being included – e.g. “Movida Locação de Veículos
S.A. was the owner/insured of the vehicle involved in the accident and is
directly liable under the insurance contract with co-defendant Seguros Sura
S.A. for the damages caused. Therefore, Movida is hereby included in the
passive pole as a necessary co-defendant.” This ties Movida into the
narrative. Keep it concise; the judge mainly needs to see that Movida is
now listed.

No need to rewrite the entire story, just reference the original
complaint’s facts as needed and assert that all claims/damages stated there
are now directed at both defendants jointly. If there are specific
allegations against Movida (for instance, negligence in some duty, or
anything Movida did or failed to do), those can be mentioned. In your
situation, Movida’s role was owning the car and holding the policy; the
primary bad behavior was by Sura (insurer) handling the claim, so Movida’s
liability may be more vicarious. It’s fine to simply include Movida due to
its legal connection, without accusing Movida of separate wrongdoing,
unless applicable.

Request for Summons: Ask the court to summon (cite) Movida S.A. to respond
to the complaint. Since Sura has already been named, the amendment will
trigger a need for Movida to be notified. The clerk will issue either a
citation via postal service or electronic means (possibly e-CNPJ if Movida
is registered for electronic service).

Attach Supporting Documents: Because you’re adding a new defendant, it
helps to attach any documents demonstrating Movida’s involvement. For
example, you should include a copy of the rental agreement or vehicle
inspection reports if you have them, and especially the insurance policy
document (apólice ou bilhete) that shows the coverage of Movida’s vehicle
by Sura. From your records, we identified relevant files like the Movida
rental contract, the pickup/return checklist, and the Sura policy/bilhete

Gmail

Gmail

. Attaching these will contextualize the Sura–Movida–you relationship,
confirming that the car was owned/insured by Movida and you are a
third-party claimant. This isn’t strictly required to amend the parties,
but it strengthens the case and preempts questions.

. It suggested naming the file e.g. “04-Emenda-Inclusao-Movida.pdf” and

. Your lawyer will prepare and file this, but you can ensure they have

Fulfilling the Other Requirements (Procuração, Documents, Evidence)

In parallel with adding Movida, don’t forget the other items the judge
listed, as they are equally important to avoid dismissal

Gmail

:

Power of Attorney: You need to provide a new “procuração ad judicia”
specific to this case number. As noted, the one from 2024 was for the other
case and wasn’t automatically carried over

Gmail

. Your attorney can generate a new power of attorney for you to sign.
Typically, you’ll sign it digitally (using e.g. the AASP Signer or another
certified method) since this is an electronic process. Once signed, the
lawyer must upload it using the designated event “PROCURAÇÃO” in the e-proc
system so that the system registers the attorney properly. (If it’s
uploaded incorrectly under a general document event, it won’t count – the
court emphasized this detail in the ordinance published on 03/11/2025.)
Essentially, make sure your lawyer knows to use the correct filing code for
the POA. Given that Carlos Magalhães has done this before, he likely knows
the procedure.

Your ID and CPF: You should scan and send a copy of your identification
document (e.g. RNE or passport, and CNH if you have Brazilian driver’s
license, etc.) plus your CPF. Courts often require a front-and-back scan of
an official photo ID and the CPF number (if not on the ID) for the case
records. Ensure these are clear and in PDF format. You indicated in email
that these were missing in your files

Gmail

, so you’ll need to locate your documents and forward them to your lawyer
or upload them if you have access. This is straightforward – just make sure
the documents are valid and not expired.

Proof of Address: Provide a recent comprovante de residência. Usually a
utility bill (water, electricity, landline, etc.) or bank statement dated
within the last 3 months is acceptable. You mentioned you have Ativo
Telecom bills in your email which could serve this purpose

Gmail

. Choose one that clearly shows your name and current address (the address

send it along. The lawyer will file it as an exhibit. We already identified
some options in your inbox for this. If needed, just forward the PDF of a

Court Fee Receipt: It’s wise to also attach the proof of payment of the
initial court costs (“custas iniciais”). The docket shows the payment (Guia
#347558, subguia 338202 for R$ 3,784.35) was made and recorded on
31/10/2025. Attaching the receipt helps demonstrate compliance. We have

Other Key Evidence: The judge’s order invites “eventuais provas com que

The insurance policy or certificate proving coverage (to link Sura and
Movida)

to substantiate the material loss claimed.

Proof of your communications with the insurer: for instance, any formal

Remember, the initial amendment doesn’t need to carry every piece of

Communications with Movida or Simpar – Do We Need More?

You asked whether we need to retrieve more emails from Simpar or Movida,
and what they might “want.” Based on our thorough search, there haven’t
been direct individual communications from Movida S.A. or Simpar (Movida’s
parent company) to you, apart from routine process emails:

Movida’s Involvement So Far: Movida was indirectly involved via the
insurance process. For example, we saw that when you sent the formal
notices, you copied sinistro@movida.com.br on the emails

Gmail

. This means Movida’s insurance/claims department was aware of your case,
but we did not find any direct reply from Movida. It’s likely Movida simply
forwarded those communications to Sura or let Sura handle it entirely. The
only “response” involving Movida we noted was the insurer’s documentation
listing Movida as the insured party. For instance, the Sura letter offering
R$48k identified “Segurado: Movida Locação de Veículos S/A” and your car as
the third-party vehicle

Google Drive

. Movida itself did not separately contact you to negotiate or deny
anything – they let Sura (and possibly Sura’s legal firm BVAA) manage the
claim. This is typical: in third-party claims, the insurer conducts the
handling, and the insured (Movida) often stays in the background unless
called into a lawsuit.

Simpar (Movida’s parent company): We found no record of you communicating
with Simpar S.A. directly. Simpar is a conglomerate and would not normally
be involved in an insurance claim dispute of its subsidiary unless you
escalated a complaint to their executive level. There was no email to or
from Simpar in your mail archive relevant to this case. So, Simpar doesn’t
factor into the court’s request – the judge specifically named Movida S.A.
There’s no indication that the court expects Simpar to be involved, and you
don’t need to include Simpar in the lawsuit (indeed, adding parties not
directly relevant could complicate matters).

In short, focus on Movida as the judge instructs. We have already gathered
the key documents linking Movida to the case (rental contract, insurance
policy, etc.). There’s no unseen email from Movida providing additional
info that we haven’t accounted for – if Movida had, say, an internal claims
correspondence with you, we’d include it, but it appears all correspondence
was channeled through Sura and their adjusters/representatives. However,
double-check your records for anything like a Movida rental confirmation or
accident report. Sometimes rental companies send an accident report or
claim form. If you towed the car or dealt with Movida’s fleet after the
crash, there might be a form or email (e.g., “Relato de Sinistro” or a
message from a Movida branch). If such a document exists and has useful
info (like acknowledging the incident, listing the car plate, etc.),
include it as well. Otherwise, the evidence you have from Sura’s side plus
the contract is sufficient.

. Once you file and fix these issues, the status will change and the case

Follow-Up: After filing, watch the docket for an entry indicating that the
